Method: 开发多视图VR远程呈现系统：1) 融合三个摄像头的几何信息，在独立VR硬件上实现GPU加速的点云渲染；2) 集成腕部安装的RGB流，在点云精度有限区域提供高分辨率局部细节；3) 在Meta Quest 3上实现约75k点的实时渲染。

Result: 31名参与者的受试者内研究显示：1) 系统在三个遥操作任务中整体性能最佳；2) 无RGB的点云模态也优于RGB流和OpenTeleVision；3) 结合全局3D结构和局部高分辨率细节显著提升操作性能。

Conclusion: 全局3D结构与局部高分辨率细节的结合显著改善了遥操作的远程呈现效果，为下一代机器人遥操作系统提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Robot teleoperation is critical for applications such as remote maintenance, fleet robotics, search and rescue, and data collection for robot learning. Effective teleoperation requires intuitive 3D visualization with reliable depth cues, which conventional screen-based interfaces often fail to provide. We introduce a multi-view VR telepresence system that (1) fuses geometry from three cameras to produce GPU-accelerated point-cloud rendering on standalone VR hardware, and (2) integrates a wrist-mounted RGB stream to provide high-resolution local detail where point-cloud accuracy is limited. Our pipeline supports real-time rendering of approximately 75k points on the Meta Quest 3. A within-subject study was conducted with 31 participants to compare our system to other visualisation modalities, such as RGB streams, a projection of stereo-vision directly in the VR device and point clouds without providing additional RGB information. Across three different teleoperated manipulation tasks, we measured task success, completion time, perceived workload, and usability. Our system achieved the best overall performance, while the Point Cloud modality without RGB also outperforming the RGB streams and OpenTeleVision. These results show that combining global 3D structure with localized high-resolution detail substantially improves telepresence for manipulation and provides a strong foundation for next-generation robot teleoperation systems.

</details>


### [150] [Build on Priors: Vision--Language--Guided Neuro-Symbolic Imitation Learning for Data-Efficient Real-World Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03759)
*Pierrick Lorang,Johannes Huemer,Timothy Duggan,Kai Goebel,Patrik Zips,Matthias Scheutz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可扩展的神经符号框架，能够从少量无标注技能演示中自主构建符号规划域和数据高效的控制策略，无需手动领域工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号方法通常依赖手工制作的符号抽象、语义标注轨迹或大型演示数据集，限制了其可扩展性和实际应用。需要一种能够从少量演示中学习长时程操作任务的方法。

Method: 框架将演示分割为技能，使用视觉语言模型（VLM）对技能分类并识别等价高层状态，自动构建状态转移图。通过答案集编程求解器合成PDDL规划域，利用预言函数为每个技能策略隔离最小化、任务相关且目标相对的动作空间。策略在控制参考层面而非原始执行器信号层面学习，利用已知控制器进行真实世界数据增强。

Result: 在真实工业叉车上进行了统计严谨的操作试验验证，并在Kinova Gen3机械臂上展示了跨平台通用性。结果表明该方法能够实现可扩展、数据高效、无需专家且可解释的神经符号机器人学习。

Conclusion: 将控制学习、VLM驱动的抽象和自动化规划合成统一到一个流程中，为可扩展、数据高效、无需专家且可解释的神经符号机器人提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Enabling robots to learn long-horizon manipulation tasks from a handful of demonstrations remains a central challenge in robotics. Existing neuro-symbolic approaches often rely on hand-crafted symbolic abstractions, semantically labeled trajectories or large demonstration datasets, limiting their scalability and real-world applicability. We present a scalable neuro-symbolic framework that autonomously constructs symbolic planning domains and data-efficient control policies from as few as one to thirty unannotated skill demonstrations, without requiring manual domain engineering. Our method segments demonstrations into skills and employs a Vision-Language Model (VLM) to classify skills and identify equivalent high-level states, enabling automatic construction of a state-transition graph. This graph is processed by an Answer Set Programming solver to synthesize a PDDL planning domain, which an oracle function exploits to isolate the minimal, task-relevant and target relative observation and action spaces for each skill policy. Policies are learned at the control reference level rather than at the raw actuator signal level, yielding a smoother and less noisy learning target. Known controllers can be leveraged for real-world data augmentation by projecting a single demonstration onto other objects in the scene, simultaneously enriching the graph construction process and the dataset for imitation learning. We validate our framework primarily on a real industrial forklift across statistically rigorous manipulation trials, and demonstrate cross-platform generality on a Kinova Gen3 robotic arm across two standard benchmarks. Our results show that grounding control learning, VLM-driven abstraction, and automated planning synthesis into a unified pipeline constitutes a practical path toward scalable, data-efficient, expert-free and interpretable neuro-symbolic robotics.

</details>


### [151] [A Novel Hybrid PID-LQR Controller for Sit-To-Stand Assistance Using a CAD-Integrated Simscape Multibody Lower Limb Exoskeleton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03766)
*Ranjeet Kumbhar,Rajmeet Singh,Appaso M Gadade,Ashish Singla,Irfan Hussain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于下肢外骨骼坐站转换的新型混合PID-LQR控制器，相比传统PID和LQR控制器，在轨迹跟踪精度、响应速度和超调控制方面表现显著优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 坐站转换过程中，人机系统的高度非线性、时变动力学特性以及临床安全对轨迹跟踪的严格要求，使得下肢外骨骼的精确控制成为康复机器人领域的核心挑战。需要开发更有效的控制策略来满足临床部署需求。

Method: 1) 从SolidWorks CAD装配体导入MATLAB/Simulink Simscape Multibody环境，构建高保真物理动力学模型；2) 使用OpenSim肌肉骨骼仿真生成生理学参考关节轨迹，并分解为三个生物力学阶段；3) 设计并比较三种控制策略：经典PID、LQR和新型混合PID-LQR控制器（通过调谐混合系数α=0.65结合LQR的最优瞬态响应和PID的积分扰动抑制）。

Result: 混合PID-LQR控制器在髋关节和膝关节的RMSE分别比PID降低72.3%和70.4%；所有关节的稳定时间相比PID减少超过90%；超调量限制在2.39%-6.10%范围内。在所有评估的性能指标上均优于两种基线策略。

Conclusion: 混合PID-LQR控制器在坐站转换任务中表现出卓越的控制性能，验证了其相对于传统控制方法的优越性，并展示了在临床辅助外骨骼部署中的强大转化潜力。

Abstract: Precise control of lower limb exoskeletons during sit-to-stand (STS) transitions remains a central challenge in rehabilitation robotics owing to the highly nonlinear, time-varying dynamics of the human-exoskeleton system and the stringent trajectory tracking requirements imposed by clinical safety. This paper presents the systematic design, simulation, and comparative evaluation of three control strategies: a classical Proportional-Integral-Derivative (PID) controller, a Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR), and a novel Hybrid PID-LQR controller applied to a bilateral lower limb exoskeleton performing the sit-to-stand transition. A high-fidelity, physics-based dynamic model of the exoskeleton is constructed by importing a SolidWorks CAD assembly directly into the MATLAB/Simulink Simscape Multibody environment, preserving accurate geometric and inertial properties of all links. Physiologically representative reference joint trajectories for the hip, knee, and ankle joints are generated using OpenSim musculoskeletal simulation and decomposed into three biomechanical phases: flexion-momentum (0-33%), momentum-transfer (34-66%), and extension (67-100%). The proposed Hybrid PID-LQR controller combines the optimal transient response of LQR with the integral disturbance rejection of PID through a tuned blending coefficient alpha = 0.65. Simulation results demonstrate that the Hybrid PID-LQR achieves RMSE reductions of 72.3% and 70.4% over PID at the hip and knee joints, respectively, reduces settling time by over 90% relative to PID across all joints, and limits overshoot to 2.39%-6.10%, confirming its superiority over both baseline strategies across all evaluated performance metrics and demonstrating strong translational potential for clinical assistive exoskeleton deployment.

</details>


### [152] [OpenRC: An Open-Source Robotic Colonoscopy Framework for Multimodal Data Acquisition and Autonomy Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03781)
*Siddhartha Kapuria,Mohammad Rafiee Javazm,Naruhiko Ikoma,Joga Ivatury,Mohammad Ali Nasseri,Nassir Navab,Farshid Alambeigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenRC是一个开源模块化机器人结肠镜框架，通过改造传统结肠镜同时保留临床工作流程，支持视频、操作命令、驱动状态和末端姿态的多模态数据同步采集，为机器人结肠镜、医学影像和视觉-语言-动作学习研究提供可重复的基础平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结肠镜平台在系统研究操作者控制、器械运动和视觉反馈耦合动态方面支持有限，限制了机器人结肠镜、医学影像和新兴视觉-语言-动作学习范式的可重复闭环研究。

Method: 开发开源模块化机器人结肠镜框架OpenRC，改造传统结肠镜同时保留临床工作流程，支持视频、操作命令、驱动状态和末端姿态的多模态数据同步记录，验证运动一致性并量化跨模态延迟。

Result: 收集了包含1,894个远程操作片段（约19小时）的多模态数据集，涵盖10种结构化任务变体，包括常规导航、失败事件和恢复行为；验证了运动一致性并量化了跨模态延迟。

Conclusion: OpenRC通过统一开放硬件和对齐的多模态数据集，为多模态机器人结肠镜和手术自主性研究提供了可重复的基础平台。

Abstract: Colorectal cancer screening critically depends on colonoscopy, yet existing platforms offer limited support for systematically studying the coupled dynamics of operator control, instrument motion, and visual feedback. This gap restricts reproducible closed-loop research in robotic colonoscopy, medical imaging, and emerging vision-language-action (VLA) learning paradigms. To address this challenge, we present OpenRC, an open-source modular robotic colonoscopy framework that retrofits conventional scopes while preserving clinical workflow. The framework supports simultaneous recording of video, operator commands, actuation state, and distal tip pose. We experimentally validated motion consistency and quantified cross-modal latency across sensing streams. Using this platform, we collected a multimodal dataset comprising 1,894 teleoperated episodes ~19 hours across 10 structured task variations of routine navigation, failure events, and recovery behaviors. By unifying open hardware and an aligned multimodal dataset, OpenRC provides a reproducible foundation for research in multimodal robotic colonoscopy and surgical autonomy.

</details>


### [153] [DC-Ada: Reward-Only Decentralized Observation-Interface Adaptation for Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03905)
*Saad Alqithami*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DC-Ada是一种用于异构多机器人团队的奖励驱动去中心化适应方法，通过调整观测变换而非微调策略来适应传感器差异，在严格步数预算下使用无梯度随机搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构性是部署多机器人团队的关键特征：平台在传感模态、范围、视场和故障模式上存在差异。在名义传感条件下训练的控制器在部署到传感器缺失或不匹配的机器人上时性能会急剧下降，即使任务和动作接口保持不变。

Method: DC-Ada保持预训练共享策略冻结，仅调整每个机器人的紧凑观测变换，将异构传感映射到固定推理接口。该方法无梯度且通信最小化：使用预算化接受/拒绝随机搜索，在严格步数预算下进行短公共随机数滚动。

Result: 在2D多机器人模拟器中评估了四个异构机制(H0-H3)和五个种子，预算为200,000个联合环境步。结果显示异构性会显著降低冻结共享策略性能，没有单一缓解方法在所有任务和指标上占优。观测归一化在仓库物流的奖励鲁棒性上最强，在搜救中具有竞争力，而冻结共享策略在协作地图构建的奖励上最强。DC-Ada提供了有用的补充操作点：在严重覆盖式地图构建中明显改善完成度，仅需标量团队回报且无需策略微调或持久通信。

Conclusion: DC-Ada作为一种实用的部署时适应方法，适用于异构团队，通过仅调整观测变换而非微调策略，在通信和计算受限的环境中提供有效的性能提升。

Abstract: Heterogeneity is a defining feature of deployed multi-robot teams: platforms often differ in sensing modalities, ranges, fields of view, and failure patterns. Controllers trained under nominal sensing can degrade sharply when deployed on robots with missing or mismatched sensors, even when the task and action interface are unchanged. We present DC-Ada, a reward-only decentralized adaptation method that keeps a pretrained shared policy frozen and instead adapts compact per-robot observation transforms to map heterogeneous sensing into a fixed inference interface. DC-Ada is gradient-free and communication-minimal: it uses budgeted accept/reject random search with short common-random-number rollouts under a strict step budget. We evaluate DC-Ada against four baselines in a deterministic 2D multi-robot simulator covering warehouse logistics, search and rescue, and collaborative mapping, across four heterogeneity regimes (H0--H3) and five seeds with a matched budget of $200{,}000$ joint environment steps per run. Results show that heterogeneity can substantially degrade a frozen shared policy and that no single mitigation dominates across all tasks and metrics. Observation normalization is strongest for reward robustness in warehouse logistics and competitive in search and rescue, while the frozen shared policy is strongest for reward in collaborative mapping. DC-Ada offers a useful complementary operating point: it improves completion most clearly in severe coverage-based mapping while requiring only scalar team returns and no policy fine-tuning or persistent communication. These results position DC-Ada as a practical deploy-time adaptation method for heterogeneous teams.

</details>


### [154] [VA-FastNavi-MARL: Real-Time Robot Control with Multimedia-Driven Meta-Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03998)
*Yang Zhang,Shengxi Jing,Fengxiang Wang,Yuan Feng,Hong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VA-FastNavi-MARL：一种基于元强化学习的异步视听对齐框架，用于实时人机交互中的动态异构多媒体指令解释


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决人机交互中动态、异构多媒体指令的实时解释问题，传统方法受限于繁重的感官处理和延迟，需要能够快速适应未见指令且保持低延迟响应的解决方案

Method: 提出VA-FastNavi-MARL框架，将异步视听输入对齐到统一的潜在表示中，通过元强化学习将多样化指令视为可导航目标的分布，实现模态无关的流处理以确保无缝低延迟控制

Result: 在多臂工作空间验证中，VA-FastNavi-MARL在样本效率上显著优于基线方法，即使在嘈杂的多媒体流下也能保持鲁棒的实时执行性能

Conclusion: 该框架通过异步视听对齐和元强化学习实现了对动态异构多媒体指令的高效实时解释，为人机交互提供了快速适应、低延迟的解决方案

Abstract: Interpreting dynamic, heterogeneous multimedia commands with real-time responsiveness is critical for Human-Robot Interaction. We present VA-FastNavi-MARL, a framework that aligns asynchronous audio-visual inputs into a unified latent representation. By treating diverse instructions as a distribution of navigable goals via Meta-Reinforcement Learning, our method enables rapid adaptation to unseen directives with negligible inference overhead. Unlike approaches bottlenecked by heavy sensory processing, our modality-agnostic stream ensures seamless, low-latency control. Validation on a multi-arm workspace confirms that VA-FastNavi-MARL significantly outperforms baselines in sample efficiency and maintains robust, real-time execution even under noisy multimedia streams.

</details>


### [155] [Dynamic Whole-Body Dancing with Humanoid Robots -- A Model-Based Control Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03999)
*Shibowen Zhang,Jiayang Wu,Guannan Liu,Helin Zhu,Junjie Liu,Zhehan Li,Junhong Guo,Xiaokun Leng,Hangxin Liu,Jingwen Zhang,Jikai Wang,Zonghai Chen,Zhicheng He,Jiayi Wang,Yao Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于模型的集成框架，用于人形机器人动态全身舞蹈动作的生成与执行，包含离线生成和在线执行两阶段，利用未来状态预测实现鲁棒动态舞蹈


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为人形机器人开发能够在真实环境中执行动态、鲁棒舞蹈动作的框架，解决从人类演示到机器人执行的全流程问题，适应现实世界干扰

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 离线阶段：通过动作捕捉获取人类舞蹈演示，通过二次规划进行重定向，轨迹优化确保动态可行性；2) 在线阶段：基于质心动力的模型预测控制框架实时跟踪计划动作，主动调整摆动脚位置适应干扰

Result: 在Kuavo 4Pro全尺寸人形机器人上验证，在仿真和四分钟公开表演中展示动态舞蹈动作，实验表明更长的预测时域能提高规划中的动作表现力和执行中的稳定性

Conclusion: 提出的集成框架成功实现了人形机器人的动态舞蹈动作生成与执行，未来状态预测对鲁棒性至关重要，为复杂动态任务提供了可行解决方案

Abstract: This paper presents an integrated model-based framework for generating and executing dynamic whole-body dance motions on humanoid robots. The framework operates in two stages: offline motion generation and online motion execution, both leveraging future state prediction to enable robust and dynamic dance motions in real-world environments. In the offline motion generation stage, human dance demonstrations are captured via a motion capture (MoCap) system, retargeted to the robot by solving a Quadratic Programming (QP) problem, and further refined using Trajectory Optimization (TO) to ensure dynamic feasibility. In the online motion execution stage, a centroidal dynamics-based Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework tracks the planned motions in real time and proactively adjusts swing foot placement to adapt to real world disturbances. We validate our framework on the full-size humanoid robot Kuavo 4Pro, demonstrating the dynamic dance motions both in simulation and in a four-minute live public performance with a team of four robots. Experimental results show that longer prediction horizons improve both motion expressiveness in planning and stability in execution.

</details>


### [156] [Learning Dexterous Grasping from Sparse Taxonomy Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04138)
*Juhan Park,Taerim Yoon,Seungmin Kim,Joonggil Kim,Wontae Ye,Jeongeun Park,Yoonbyung Chai,Geonwoo Cho,Geunwoo Cho,Dohyeong Kim,Kyungjae Lee,Yongjae Kim,Sungjoon Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRIT是一个两阶段框架，通过学习稀疏分类学指导来实现灵巧操作，首先预测基于分类学的抓取规范，然后基于此生成连续手指动作，在保持预期抓取结构的同时完成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法需要为每个物体和任务指定密集的位姿或接触目标，这在实际中不可行；而端到端的强化学习缺乏可控性，用户难以在失败时进行干预。需要一种既能从任务奖励中学习，又具有可控性的方法。

Method: GRIT采用两阶段框架：第一阶段从场景和任务上下文中预测基于分类学的稀疏抓取规范；第二阶段基于这个稀疏指令，通过策略生成连续的手指动作，在完成任务的同时保持预期的抓取结构。

Result: 实验表明某些抓取分类学对特定物体几何更有效。利用这种关系，GRIT在新物体上的泛化能力优于基线方法，总体成功率达到87.9%。真实世界实验证明了可控性，用户可以通过基于物体几何和任务意图的高层分类学选择来调整抓取策略。

Conclusion: GRIT通过结合分类学指导和强化学习，实现了既具有学习能力又保持可控性的灵巧操作框架，能够适应不同物体几何并允许用户干预，为实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation requires planning a grasp configuration suited to the object and task, which is then executed through coordinated multi-finger control. However, specifying grasp plans with dense pose or contact targets for every object and task is impractical. Meanwhile, end-to-end reinforcement learning from task rewards alone lacks controllability, making it difficult for users to intervene when failures occur. To this end, we present GRIT, a two-stage framework that learns dexterous control from sparse taxonomy guidance. GRIT first predicts a taxonomy-based grasp specification from the scene and task context. Conditioned on this sparse command, a policy generates continuous finger motions that accomplish the task while preserving the intended grasp structure. Our result shows that certain grasp taxonomies are more effective for specific object geometries. By leveraging this relationship, GRIT improves generalization to novel objects over baselines and achieves an overall success rate of 87.9%. Moreover, real-world experiments demonstrate controllability, enabling grasp strategies to be adjusted through high-level taxonomy selection based on object geometry and task intent.

</details>


### [157] [Adaptive Action Chunking at Inference-time for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04161)
*Yuanchang Liang,Xiaobo Wang,Kai Wang,Shuo Wang,Xiaojiang Peng,Haoyu Chen,David Kim Huat Chua,Prahlad Vadakkepat*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自适应动作分块策略，利用动作熵自适应确定分块大小，平衡VLA模型的响应性和一致性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型在推理时使用固定的分块长度，无法在响应性和一致性之间取得平衡，限制了模型在不同操作任务中的优越性和可扩展性

Method: 提出自适应动作分块策略，利用动作熵作为线索，基于当前预测自适应确定分块大小

Result: 在广泛的模拟和真实世界机器人操作任务中，该方法显著优于现有最先进方法

Conclusion: 自适应动作分块策略能有效解决VLA模型中分块大小选择的难题，提升机器人操作性能

Abstract: In Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, action chunking (i.e., executing a sequence of actions without intermediate replanning) is a key technique to improve robotic manipulation abilities. However, a large chunk size reduces the model's responsiveness to new information, while a small one increases the likelihood of mode-jumping, jerky behavior resulting from discontinuities between chunks. Therefore, selecting the optimal chunk size is an urgent demand to balance the model's reactivity and consistency. Unfortunately, a dominant trend in current VLA models is an empirical fixed chunk length at inference-time, hindering their superiority and scalability across diverse manipulation tasks. To address this issue, we propose a novel Adaptive Action Chunking (AAC) strategy, which exploits action entropy as the cue to adaptively determine the chunk size based on current predictions. Extensive experiments on a wide range of simulated and real-world robotic manipulation tasks have demonstrated that our approach substantially improves performance over the state-of-the-art alternatives. The videos and source code are publicly available at https://lance-lot.github.io/adaptive-chunking.github.io/.

</details>


### [158] [Primitive-based Truncated Diffusion for Efficient Trajectory Generation of Differential Drive Mobile Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04166)
*Long Xu,Choilam Wong,Yuhang Zhong,Junxiao Lin,Jialiang Hou,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于差速驱动移动机械臂的学习增强运动规划器，通过关键点序列提取和基于基元的截断扩散模型提高规划效率、成功率和最优性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运动规划方法在复杂3D环境中存在效率低、成功率不足和轨迹多样性有限的问题，需要结合学习技术提升移动机械臂的运动规划性能

Method: 1) 关键点序列提取模块：通过可微分正向运动学将边界状态映射到3D空间；2) 点云和关键点分别编码并通过注意力机制融合；3) 基于基元的截断扩散模型：从偏置分布中采样，相比原始扩散模型提高效率和多样性；4) 去噪路径通过轨迹优化确保动态可行性和任务特定最优性

Result: 在杂乱3D仿真环境中，相比原始扩散模型和经典基线方法，该方法实现了更高的成功率、改进的轨迹多样性和具有竞争力的运行时间

Conclusion: 提出的学习增强运动规划器通过结合关键点序列编码和基于基元的截断扩散模型，有效提升了移动机械臂在复杂环境中的运动规划性能，在效率、成功率和轨迹多样性方面均取得显著改进

Abstract: We present a learning-enhanced motion planner for differential drive mobile manipulators to improve efficiency, success rate, and optimality. For task representation encoder, we propose a keypoint sequence extraction module that maps boundary states to 3D space via differentiable forward kinematics. Point clouds and keypoints are encoded separately and fused with attention, enabling effective integration of environment and boundary states information. We also propose a primitive-based truncated diffusion model that samples from a biased distribution. Compared with vanilla diffusion model, this framework improves the efficiency and diversity of the solution. Denoised paths are refined by trajectory optimization to ensure dynamic feasibility and task-specific optimality. In cluttered 3D simulations, our method achieves higher success rate, improved trajectory diversity, and competitive runtime compared to vanilla diffusion and classical baselines. The source code is released at https://github.com/nmoma/nmoma .

</details>


### [159] [Robots Need Some Education: On the complexity of learning in evolutionary robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04196)
*Fuda van Diggelen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了进化机器人与机器人学习两个领域的区别与整合，重点研究了在进化机器人框架下设计合适学习算法的挑战，并提出了几种适用于该背景的学习算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化机器人和机器人学习都旨在自动优化机器人设计，但两者在优化对象和时间尺度上存在差异。将机器人学习整合到进化机器人中需要精心设计合适的学习算法，而引入学习对进化过程的影响尚未被充分理解，这带来了研究挑战。

Method: 论文研究了在进化机器人背景下整合学习算法的复杂性，并开发了多种专门针对进化机器人环境设计的学习算法。

Result: 论文提出了几种适用于进化机器人背景的学习算法，深入探讨了学习与进化整合过程中的技术细节和挑战。

Conclusion: 成功整合机器人学习与进化机器人需要精心设计的学习算法，理解学习对进化过程的影响是关键挑战，论文为此提供了算法解决方案和深入分析。

Abstract: Evolutionary Robotics and Robot Learning are two fields in robotics that aim to automatically optimize robot designs. The key difference between them lies in what is being optimized and the time scale involved. Evolutionary Robotics is a field that applies evolutionary computation techniques to evolve the morphologies or controllers, or both. Robot Learning, on the other hand, involves any learning technique aimed at optimizing a robot's controller in a given morphology. In terms of time scales, evolution occurs across multiple generations, whereas learning takes place within the `lifespan' of an individual robot. Integrating Robot Learning with Evolutionary Robotics requires the careful design of suitable learning algorithms in the context of evolutionary robotics. The effects of introducing learning into the evolutionary process are not well-understood and can thus be tricky. This thesis investigates these intricacies and presents several learning algorithms developed for an Evolutionary Robotics context.

</details>


### [160] [RK-MPC: Residual Koopman Model Predictive Control for Quadruped Locomotion in Offroad Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04221)
*Sriram S. K. S. Narayanan,Umesh Vaidya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Residual Koopman MPC框架，通过数据驱动的残差学习提升四足机器人运动预测精度，在保持实时性的同时实现复杂地形下的鲁棒控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模板模型在接触变化和地形扰动下存在模型失配问题，需要提升预测精度同时保持实时计算能力，以应对非结构化环境中的四足机器人控制挑战

Method: 在名义模板模型基础上，通过数据学习紧凑的线性残差预测器（在提升坐标中），将残差模型嵌入凸二次规划MPC框架，实现500Hz的板载实时控制

Result: 在Gazebo仿真和Unitree Go1硬件实验中，实现了对接触扰动、多种步态和复杂地形（草地、碎石、雪地、冰面）的可靠盲运动控制，相比Koopman/EDMD基线方法，残差校正提升了多步预测和闭环性能

Conclusion: RK-MPC为四足机器人在非结构化环境中的数据驱动预测控制提供了实用且经过硬件验证的途径，在保持优化控制结构优势的同时，通过残差学习有效提升了模型预测精度

Abstract: This paper presents Residual Koopman MPC (RK-MPC), a Koopman-based, data-driven model predictive control framework for quadruped locomotion that improves prediction fidelity while preserving real-time tractability. RK-MPC augments a nominal template model with a compact linear residual predictor learned from data in lifted coordinates, enabling systematic correction of model mismatch induced by contact variability and terrain disturbances with provable bounds on multi-step prediction error. The learned residual model is embedded within a convex quadratic-program MPC formulation, yielding a receding-horizon controller that runs onboard at 500 Hz and retains the structure and constraint-handling advantages of optimization-based control. We evaluate RK-MPC in both Gazebo simulation and Unitree Go1 hardware experiments, demonstrating reliable blind locomotion across contact disturbances, multiple gait schedules, and challenging off-road terrains including grass, gravel, snow, and ice. We further compare against Koopman/EDMD baselines using alternative observable dictionaries, including monomial and $SE(3)$-structured bases, and show that the residual correction improves multi-step prediction and closed-loop performance while reducing sensitivity to the choice of observables. Overall, RK-MPC provides a practical, hardware-validated pathway for data-driven predictive control of quadrupeds in unstructured environments. See https://sriram-2502.github.io/rk-mpc for implementation videos.

</details>


### [161] [frax: Fast Robot Kinematics and Dynamics in JAX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04310)
*Daniel Morton,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: frax是一个基于JAX的机器人运动学和动力学库，提供跨CPU、GPU和TPU的高性能纯Python接口，支持实时控制和并行化，在CPU上实现微秒级计算，在GPU上达到每秒1亿次动力学评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人动力学库通常在低延迟CPU执行或高吞吐GPU工作负载方面表现优异，但缺乏一个统一框架能够在多种架构上同时保持高性能和易用性。需要一种既能满足实时控制需求，又能支持大规模并行计算的解决方案。

Method: 采用基于JAX的纯Python实现，通过完全向量化的机器人动力学方法，支持自动微分。该库针对CPU、GPU和TPU进行优化，提供统一的编程接口，实现跨架构的高性能计算。

Result: 在CPU上实现微秒级计算时间，适合千赫兹控制频率，性能优于常见Python库并接近优化的C++实现。在GPU上，相同代码可扩展到数千个实例，达到每秒超过1亿次动力学评估。在Franka Panda机械臂和Unitree G1人形机器人上验证了性能。

Conclusion: frax成功解决了现有机器人动力学库在跨架构性能和易用性方面的不足，提供了一个高性能、易用且兼容多种硬件的统一框架，支持实时控制、并行计算和基于优化的方法，已作为开源库发布。

Abstract: In robot control, planning, and learning, there is a need for rigid-body dynamics libraries that are highly performant, easy to use, and compatible with CPUs and accelerators. While existing libraries often excel at either low-latency CPU execution or high-throughput GPU workloads, few provide a unified framework that targets multiple architectures without compromising performance or ease-of-use. To address this, we introduce frax, a JAX-based library for robot kinematics and dynamics, providing a high-performance, pure-Python interface across CPU, GPU, and TPU. Via a fully-vectorized approach to robot dynamics, frax enables efficient real-time control and parallelization, while supporting automatic differentiation for optimization-based methods. On CPU, frax achieves low-microsecond computation times suitable for kilohertz control rates, outperforming common libraries in Python and approaching optimized C++ implementations. On GPU, the same code scales to thousands of instances, reaching upwards of 100 million dynamics evaluations per second. We validate performance on a Franka Panda manipulator and a Unitree G1 humanoid, and release frax as an open-source library.

</details>


### [162] [Towards Considerate Human-Robot Coexistence: A Dual-Space Framework of Robot Design and Human Perception in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04374)
*Yuanchen Bai,Zijian Ding,Ruixiang Han,Niti Parikh,Wendy Ju,Angelique Taylor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过深度访谈探讨人机共存中人类感知的动态演变，提出人类感知空间的概念框架，并建立人类感知空间与机器人设计空间之间的协同演化循环模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人能力扩展、交互更直观、更深入现实工作流程，人机共存正从简单的物理空间共享转变为具有组织嵌入性、时间演化性、社会情境性和开放不确定性的复杂关系。然而，先前研究主要关注态度和接受的静态快照，缺乏对人类感知如何形成和演变，以及人类在塑造动态共存过程中所起积极作用的深入理解。

Method: 通过对14周医疗机器人协同设计研究中9名参与者进行深度后续访谈，识别人类感知空间，包括四个解释维度：分解程度、时间取向、推理范围和证据来源。建立人类感知空间与机器人设计空间之间的协同演化循环概念框架。

Result: 识别出人类感知空间的四个解释维度，并构建了人类感知空间与机器人设计空间之间的协同演化循环模型。该模型表明人类需求、设计决策、情境解释和社会调解在时间维度上持续相互重塑。在此基础上提出了"体贴的人机共存"概念，强调人类不仅是设计贡献者，更是解释者和调解者，主动塑造机器人如何在不同部署阶段被理解和整合。

Conclusion: 研究丰富了人机共存的概念框架，强调人类在动态共存过程中的主动作用。提出的协同演化循环模型为理解人机关系的动态性提供了新视角，而"体贴的人机共存"概念则强调了人类作为解释者和调解者的关键角色，为未来机器人设计和部署提供了重要启示。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of robotics, spanning expanded capabilities, more intuitive interaction, and more integration into real-world workflows, is reshaping what it means for humans and robots to coexist. Beyond sharing physical space, this coexistence is increasingly characterized by organizational embeddedness, temporal evolution, social situatedness, and open-ended uncertainty. However, prior work has largely focused on static snapshots of attitudes and acceptance, offering limited insight into how perceptions form and evolve, and what active role humans play in shaping coexistence as a dynamic process. We address these gaps through in-depth follow-up interviews with nine participants from a 14-week co-design study on healthcare robots. We identify the human perception space, including four interpretive dimensions (i.e., degree of decomposition, temporal orientation, scope of reasoning, and source of evidence). We enrich the conceptual framework of human-robot coexistence by conceptualizing the mutual relationship between the human perception space and the robot design space as a co-evolving loop, in which human needs, design decisions, situated interpretations, and social mediation continuously reshape one another over time. Building on this, we propose considerate human-robot coexistence, arguing that humans act not only as design contributors but also as interpreters and mediators who actively shape how robots are understood and integrated across deployment stages.

</details>


### [163] [ReinVBC: A Model-based Reinforcement Learning Approach to Vehicle Braking Controller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04401)
*Haoxin Lin,Junjie Zhou,Daheng Xu,Yang Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ReinVBC方法，将离线模型强化学习应用于车辆制动控制，通过工程化设计获得可靠的车辆动力学模型和制动策略，有望替代生产级防抱死制动系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前车辆制动系统在生产过程中需要大量人工标定，如何在保持车辆制动控制器性能的同时减少劳动力和时间消耗对汽车行业具有重要意义。基于模型的离线强化学习方法为处理现实世界控制任务提供了有前景的解决方案

Method: 提出ReinVBC方法，采用离线模型强化学习处理车辆制动控制问题。在模型学习和利用范式中引入有用的工程化设计，以获得可靠的车辆动力学模型和有效的制动策略

Result: 多个结果表明该方法在真实世界车辆制动中具有良好能力，并显示出替代生产级防抱死制动系统的潜力

Conclusion: ReinVBC通过离线模型强化学习方法成功应用于车辆制动控制，减少了人工标定需求，在保持性能的同时提高了效率，为汽车制动系统的智能化升级提供了可行方案

Abstract: Braking system, the key module to ensure the safety and steer-ability of current vehicles, relies on extensive manual calibration during production. Reducing labor and time consumption while maintaining the Vehicle Braking Controller (VBC) performance greatly benefits the vehicle industry. Model-based methods in offline reinforcement learning, which facilitate policy exploration within a data-driven dynamics model, offer a promising solution for addressing real-world control tasks. This work proposes ReinVBC, which applies an offline model-based reinforcement learning approach to deal with the vehicle braking control problem. We introduce useful engineering designs into the paradigm of model learning and utilization to obtain a reliable vehicle dynamics model and a capable braking policy. Several results demonstrate the capability of our method in real-world vehicle braking and its potential to replace the production-grade anti-lock braking system.

</details>


### [164] [Veo-Act: How Far Can Frontier Video Models Advance Generalizable Robot Manipulation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04502)
*Zhongru Zhang,Chenghan Yang,Qingzhou Lu,Yanjiang Guo,Jianke Zhang,Yucheng Hu,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究探索先进视频生成模型Veo-3在机器人操作中的泛化能力，提出零射方法Veo-3+IDM和分层框架Veo-Act，显著提升视觉-语言-动作策略的指令跟随性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型在物理动态理解方面进展迅速，本研究旨在探索先进视频生成模型（如Veo-3）如何支持泛化的机器人操作，特别是通过零射方法将视觉预测转化为可执行的机器人动作

Method: 1. 零射方法Veo-3+IDM：使用Veo-3从当前机器人观测预测未来图像序列，同时训练逆动力学模型IDM从随机游戏数据中恢复对应机器人动作；2. 分层框架Veo-Act：使用Veo-3作为高层运动规划器，视觉-语言-动作策略作为低层执行器

Result: Veo-3+IDM能在仿真和真实世界的高维灵巧手任务中生成大致正确的任务级轨迹，但低层控制精度不足；Veo-Act框架显著提升了最先进视觉-语言-动作策略的指令跟随性能

Conclusion: 随着视频生成模型的持续改进，视频模型可以成为泛化机器人学习的有价值组件，分层方法结合了视频模型的高层规划能力和专业策略的低层执行能力

Abstract: Video generation models have advanced rapidly and are beginning to show a strong understanding of physical dynamics. In this paper, we investigate how far an advanced video generation model such as Veo-3 can support generalizable robotic manipulation. We first study a zero-shot approach in which Veo-3 predicts future image sequences from current robot observations, while an inverse dynamics model IDM recovers the corresponding robot actions. The IDM is trained solely on random-play data, requiring neither human supervision nor expert demonstrations. The key intuition is that, if a video model can generate physically plausible future motions in image space, an IDM can translate those visual trajectories into executable robot actions. We evaluate this "Veo-3+IDM" approach in both simulation and the real world using a high-dimensional dexterous hand. We find that, owing to the strong generalization capability of frontier video models, Veo-3+IDM can consistently generate approximately correct task-level trajectories. However, its low-level control accuracy remains insufficient to solve most tasks reliably. Motivated by this observation, we develop a hierarchical framework, Veo-Act, which uses Veo-3 as a high-level motion planner and a VLA policy as the low-level executor, significantly improving the instruction-following performance of a state-of-the-art vision-language-action policy. Overall, our results suggest that, as video generation models continue to improve, video models can be a valuable component for generalizable robot learning.

</details>


### [165] [G-EDF-Loc: 3D Continuous Gaussian Distance Field for Robust Gradient-Based 6DoF Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04525)
*José E. Maese,Lucía Coto-Elena,Luis Merino,Fernando Caballero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于G-EDF（连续内存高效3D距离场）的6-DoF直接扫描到地图配准定位框架，在CPU上实现实时高性能定位


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种能够在CPU上实现实时6-DoF定位的鲁棒系统，特别是在里程计退化或缺乏IMU先验的挑战性场景下保持性能

Method: 采用G-EDF（基于块稀疏高斯混合模型的自适应空间划分连续距离场），保持C^1连续性和Eikonal一致性，利用解析梯度进行直接扫描到地图配准

Result: 在大规模数据集上表现出与最先进方法相当的性能，在严重里程计退化或完全缺乏IMU先验的情况下展现出卓越的鲁棒性

Conclusion: G-EDF-Loc框架通过连续内存高效的距离场表示实现了鲁棒的实时6-DoF定位，为挑战性环境下的定位问题提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: This paper presents a robust 6-DoF localization framework based on a direct, CPU-based scan-to-map registration pipeline. The system leverages G-EDF, a novel continuous and memory-efficient 3D distance field representation. The approach models the Euclidean Distance Field (EDF) using a Block-Sparse Gaussian Mixture Model with adaptive spatial partitioning, ensuring $C^1$ continuity across block transitions and mitigating boundary artifacts. By leveraging the analytical gradients of this continuous map, which maintain Eikonal consistency, the proposed method achieves high-fidelity spatial reconstruction and real-time localization. Experimental results on large-scale datasets demonstrate that G-EDF-Loc performs competitively against state-of-the-art methods, exhibiting exceptional resilience even under severe odometry degradation or in the complete absence of IMU priors.

</details>


### [166] [Visual Prompt Based Reasoning for Offroad Mapping using Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04564)
*Abdelmoamen Nasser,Yousef Baba'a,Murad Mebrahtu,Nadya Abdel Madjid,Jorge Dias,Majid Khonji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种零样本越野自主导航方法，利用SAM2进行环境分割和视觉语言模型(VLM)推理可行驶区域，无需单独训练地形分类、高度估计等模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统越野自主导航方法需要分别训练地形分类、高度估计和滑移/坡度量化等多个模型，这需要单独训练每个组件、特定任务的数据集和微调，过程复杂且效率低

Method: 使用SAM2进行环境分割，将原始图像和带有数字标签标注每个掩码的分割图像输入VLM，通过提示让VLM识别哪些数字标签对应的区域是可行驶的，结合规划和控制系统形成统一框架

Result: 该方法在高分辨率分割数据集上超越了最先进的可训练模型，并在Isaac Sim越野环境中实现了完整的堆栈导航

Conclusion: 该统一框架消除了对显式地形特定模型的需求，依靠VLM的固有推理能力实现越野自主导航，提供了一种更高效的零样本方法

Abstract: Traditional approaches to off-road autonomy rely on separate models for terrain classification, height estimation, and quantifying slip or slope conditions. Utilizing several models requires training each component separately, having task specific datasets, and fine-tuning. In this work, we present a zero-shot approach leveraging SAM2 for environment segmentation and a vision-language model (VLM) to reason about drivable areas. Our approach involves passing to the VLM both the original image and the segmented image annotated with numeric labels for each mask. The VLM is then prompted to identify which regions, represented by these numeric labels, are drivable. Combined with planning and control modules, this unified framework eliminates the need for explicit terrain-specific models and relies instead on the inherent reasoning capabilities of the VLM. Our approach surpasses state-of-the-art trainable models on high resolution segmentation datasets and enables full stack navigation in our Isaac Sim offroad environment.

</details>


### [167] [Biologically Inspired Event-Based Perception and Sample-Efficient Learning for High-Speed Table Tennis Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04618)
*Ziqi Wang,Jingyue Zhao,Xun Xiao,Jichao Yang,Yaohua Wang,Shi Xu,Lei Wang,Huadong Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种受生物启发的乒乓球机器人系统，结合事件感知与样本高效学习，解决高速动态场景下的感知与决策挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于帧的视觉传感器在高速动态场景中存在运动模糊、高延迟和数据冗余问题，无法满足实时准确感知需求；现有事件感知方法局限于简化的球体场景，决策方法需要大量环境交互导致计算成本高

Method: 1) 事件感知：提出基于运动线索和几何一致性的事件球体检测方法，直接在异步事件流上操作，无需帧重建；2) 决策：采用人类启发的样本高效训练策略，先在低速场景训练策略，逐步获取从基础到高级技能，然后通过案例依赖的时间自适应奖励和奖励阈值机制适应高速场景

Result: 在相同训练回合数下，返回目标精度提高35.8%，证明了生物启发感知与决策方法在高速机器人系统中的有效性

Conclusion: 结合事件感知与样本高效学习的生物启发方法能够有效解决高速动态场景下的机器人感知与决策挑战，为高速机器人系统提供了有前景的解决方案

Abstract: Perception and decision-making in high-speed dynamic scenarios remain challenging for current robots. In contrast, humans and animals can rapidly perceive and make decisions in such environments. Taking table tennis as a typical example, conventional frame-based vision sensors suffer from motion blur, high latency and data redundancy, which can hardly meet real-time, accurate perception requirements. Inspired by the human visual system, event-based perception methods address these limitations through asynchronous sensing, high temporal resolution, and inherently sparse data representations. However, current event-based methods are still restricted to simplified, unrealistic ball-only scenarios. Meanwhile, existing decision-making approaches typically require thousands of interactions with the environment to converge, resulting in significant computational costs. In this work, we present a biologically inspired approach for high-speed table tennis robots, combining event-based perception with sample-efficient learning. On the perception side, we propose an event-based ball detection method that leverages motion cues and geometric consistency, operating directly on asynchronous event streams without frame reconstruction, to achieve robust and efficient detection in real-world rallies. On the decision-making side, we introduce a human-inspired, sample-efficient training strategy that first trains policies in low-speed scenarios, progressively acquiring skills from basic to advanced, and then adapts them to high-speed scenarios, guided by a case-dependent temporally adaptive reward and a reward-threshold mechanism. With the same training episodes, our method improves return-to-target accuracy by 35.8%. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of biologically inspired perception and decision-making for high-speed robotic systems.

</details>


### [168] [WaterSplat-SLAM: Photorealistic Monocular SLAM in Underwater Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04642)
*Kangxu Wang,Shaofeng Zou,Chenxing Jiang,Yixiang Dai,Siang Chen,Shaojie Shen,Guijin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WaterSplat-SLAM：一种新颖的单目水下SLAM系统，通过语义介质滤波和自适应高斯地图实现鲁棒位姿估计和逼真密集建图


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水下SLAM方法难以生成高保真渲染地图，而水下单目SLAM在自主水下航行器和海洋考古等领域有重要应用需求

Method: 1. 将语义介质滤波耦合到两视图3D重建先验中，实现水下自适应相机跟踪和深度估计；2. 提出语义引导渲染和自适应地图管理策略，使用在线介质感知高斯地图以逼真紧凑方式建模水下环境

Result: 在多个水下数据集上的实验表明，WaterSplat-SLAM能够在水下环境中实现鲁棒的相机跟踪和高保真渲染

Conclusion: WaterSplat-SLAM通过结合语义介质滤波和自适应高斯地图，成功解决了水下SLAM中位姿估计和高质量渲染的挑战，为水下环境感知提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Underwater monocular SLAM is a challenging problem with applications from autonomous underwater vehicles to marine archaeology. However, existing underwater SLAM methods struggle to produce maps with high-fidelity rendering. In this paper, we propose WaterSplat-SLAM, a novel monocular underwater SLAM system that achieves robust pose estimation and photorealistic dense mapping. Specifically, we couple semantic medium filtering into two-view 3D reconstruction prior to enable underwater-adapted camera tracking and depth estimation. Furthermore, we present a semantic-guided rendering and adaptive map management strategy with an online medium-aware Gaussian map, modeling underwater environment in a photorealistic and compact manner. Experiments on multiple underwater datasets demonstrate that WaterSplat-SLAM achieves robust camera tracking and high-fidelity rendering in underwater environments.

</details>


### [169] [Pickalo: Leveraging 6D Pose Estimation for Low-Cost Industrial Bin Picking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04690)
*Alessandro Tarsi,Matteo Mastrogiuseppe,Saverio Taliani,Simone Cortinovis,Ugo Pattacini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Pickalo是一个基于低成本硬件的模块化6D位姿抓取系统，通过主动多视角探索、改进深度估计和零样本姿态估计，在密集工业环境中实现高效稳定的抓取性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业环境中的箱体抓取面临严重遮挡、杂乱和高成本3D传感的挑战，需要开发基于低成本硬件的鲁棒抓取解决方案。

Method: 系统采用手腕安装的RGB-D相机进行主动多视角探索，使用BridgeDepth改进深度估计，Mask-RCNN进行实例分割，SAM-6D进行零样本姿态估计，姿态缓冲模块融合多视角观测，离线生成抓取候选并在线进行效用排序和碰撞检测。

Result: 在UR5e机械臂和Intel RealSense D435i上部署，Pickalo达到每小时600次平均抓取，成功率96-99%，在密集填充的欧标箱上实现30分钟稳定运行。

Conclusion: Pickalo证明了低成本硬件结合先进算法能够在真实工业环境中实现高效稳定的箱体抓取，改进的深度估计和姿态缓冲对长期稳定性和吞吐量有显著提升。

Abstract: Bin picking in real industrial environments remains challenging due to severe clutter, occlusions, and the high cost of traditional 3D sensing setups. We present Pickalo, a modular 6D pose-based bin-picking pipeline built entirely on low-cost hardware. A wrist-mounted RGB-D camera actively explores the scene from multiple viewpoints, while raw stereo streams are processed with BridgeDepth to obtain refined depth maps suitable for accurate collision reasoning. Object instances are segmented with a Mask-RCNN model trained purely on photorealistic synthetic data and localized using the zero-shot SAM-6D pose estimator. A pose buffer module fuses multi-view observations over time, handling object symmetries and significantly reducing pose noise. Offline, we generate and curate large sets of antipodal grasp candidates per object; online, a utility-based ranking and fast collision checking are queried for the grasp planning. Deployed on a UR5e with a parallel-jaw gripper and an Intel RealSense D435i, Pickalo achieves up to 600 mean picks per hour with 96-99% grasp success and robust performance over 30-minute runs on densely filled euroboxes. Ablation studies demonstrate the benefits of enhanced depth estimation and of the pose buffer for long-term stability and throughput in realistic industrial conditions. Videos are available at https://mesh-iit.github.io/project-jl2-camozzi/

</details>


### [170] [AnyUser: Translating Sketched User Intent into Domestic Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04811)
*Songyuan Yang,Huibin Tan,Kailun Yang,Wenjing Yang,Shaowu Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnyUser是一个统一的机器人指令系统，通过相机图像上的自由形式草图（可选结合语言）实现直观的家庭任务指令，将多模态输入解释为空间语义基元来生成可执行动作，无需先验地图或模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高级机器人能力与非专家用户可访问交互之间的差距，为适应现实世界人类环境的实用辅助机器人奠定基础，使非专业用户能够直观地指导机器人执行家庭任务。

Method: 采用多模态融合理解方法，将草图、视觉和语言输入解释为空间语义基元，结合分层策略进行鲁棒的动作生成，系统无需先验地图或模型即可工作。

Result: 在大规模数据集上的定量基准测试显示高精度解释能力；在两个不同机器人平台上的真实世界验证（静态安装的7-DoF辅助臂和双臂移动操作器）成功执行了针对性擦拭和区域清洁等任务；用户研究显示高任务完成率（85.7%-96.4%）和用户满意度。

Conclusion: AnyUser通过自由形式草图结合语言的多模态交互，有效弥合了机器人能力与非专家用户可访问性之间的差距，为实用辅助机器人系统提供了可行方案，展示了在现实世界环境中可靠执行任务的能力。

Abstract: We introduce AnyUser, a unified robotic instruction system for intuitive domestic task instruction via free-form sketches on camera images, optionally with language. AnyUser interprets multimodal inputs (sketch, vision, language) as spatial-semantic primitives to generate executable robot actions requiring no prior maps or models. Novel components include multimodal fusion for understanding and a hierarchical policy for robust action generation. Efficacy is shown via extensive evaluations: (1) Quantitative benchmarks on the large-scale dataset showing high accuracy in interpreting diverse sketch-based commands across various simulated domestic scenes. (2) Real-world validation on two distinct robotic platforms, a statically mounted 7-DoF assistive arm (KUKA LBR iiwa) and a dual-arm mobile manipulator (Realman RMC-AIDAL), performing representative tasks like targeted wiping and area cleaning, confirming the system's ability to ground instructions and execute them reliably in physical environments. (3) A comprehensive user study involving diverse demographics (elderly, simulated non-verbal, low technical literacy) demonstrating significant improvements in usability and task specification efficiency, achieving high task completion rates (85.7%-96.4%) and user satisfaction. AnyUser bridges the gap between advanced robotic capabilities and the need for accessible non-expert interaction, laying the foundation for practical assistive robots adaptable to real-world human environments.

</details>


### [171] [Efficient Multi-Objective Planning with Weighted Maximization Using Large Neighbourhood Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04826)
*Krishna Kalavadia,Shamak Dutta,Yash Vardhan Pant,Stephen L. Smith*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于大邻域搜索框架的新算法，高效解决加权最大值规划问题，相比现有方法实现1-2个数量级的运行时间改进


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主导航需要同时优化多个目标。传统加权和方法无法找到所有可能的权衡方案，会遗漏关键解。加权最大值方法能找到所有帕累托最优解，包括加权和方法无法找到的非凸区域解，但其在离散域中的计算复杂度限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出基于大邻域搜索框架的新型搜索算法，用于高效解决加权最大值规划问题。

Result: 通过大量仿真验证，该算法在解质量上与现有加权最大值规划器相当，但运行时间提高了1-2个数量级，使其成为自主导航的可行选择。

Conclusion: 提出的基于大邻域搜索的算法显著提高了加权最大值规划的计算效率，使其能够实际应用于自主导航任务，解决了传统方法无法找到所有帕累托最优解的问题。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation often requires the simultaneous optimization of multiple objectives. The most common approach scalarizes these into a single cost function using a weighted sum, but this method is unable to find all possible trade-offs and can therefore miss critical solutions. An alternative, the weighted maximum of objectives, can find all Pareto-optimal solutions, including those in non-convex regions of the trade-off space that weighted sum methods cannot find. However, the increased computational complexity of finding weighted maximum solutions in the discrete domain has limited its practical use. To address this challenge, we propose a novel search algorithm based on the Large Neighbourhood Search framework that efficiently solves the weighted maximum planning problem. Through extensive simulations, we demonstrate that our algorithm achieves comparable solution quality to existing weighted maximum planners with a runtime improvement of 1-2 orders of magnitude, making it a viable option for autonomous navigation.

</details>


### [172] [Outlier-Robust Nonlinear Moving Horizon Estimation using Adaptive Loss Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04862)
*Nestor Deniz,Guido Sanchez,Fernando Auat Cheein,Leonardo Giovanini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用于移动视窗估计的自适应鲁棒损失函数框架，通过自适应鲁棒损失减少异常值影响，加入正则化项避免朴素解，优先拟合未污染数据并降低污染数据权重


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统移动视窗估计对异常值敏感，需要鲁棒方法来处理测量数据中的异常值，同时保持对无异常值数据的良好性能

Method: 集成自适应鲁棒损失函数和正则化项，通过调节参数控制损失函数形状以调整对异常值的鲁棒性，优先拟合未污染数据并降低污染数据权重

Result: 仿真结果表明自适应过程在几次迭代内完成，当测量数据无异常值时表现出传统L2范数的行为

Conclusion: 提出的自适应鲁棒损失函数框架能有效处理移动视窗估计中的异常值问题，在保持对干净数据良好性能的同时提供对异常值的鲁棒性

Abstract: In this work, we propose an adaptive robust loss function framework for MHE, integrating an adaptive robust loss function to reduce the impact of outliers with a regularization term that avoids naive solutions. The proposed approach prioritizes the fitting of uncontaminated data and downweights the contaminated ones. A tuning parameter is incorporated into the framework to control the shape of the loss function for adjusting the estimator's robustness to outliers. The simulation results demonstrate that adaptation occurs in just a few iterations, whereas the traditional behaviour $\mathrm{L_2}$ predominates when the measurements are free of outliers.

</details>


### [173] [Copilot-Assisted Second-Thought Framework for Brain-to-Robot Hand Motion Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27492)
*Yizhe Li,Shixiao Wang,Jian K. Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种CNN-注意力混合模型，用于从脑电图解码手部运动学，并在抓握任务中表现出色。进一步扩展到EEG-EMG多模态解码，显著提升性能。还引入了copilot框架通过运动状态感知的critic过滤低置信度解码点，提高轨迹保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从脑电图（EEG）预测运动学是开发运动相关脑机接口的重要研究方向。传统方法通常依赖CNN或RNN，而基于Transformer的模型在处理长序列EEG数据方面表现出强大能力。本研究旨在开发更有效的EEG解码方法，并探索多模态融合（EEG-EMG）以提升解码性能。

Method: 提出CNN-注意力混合模型用于解码手部运动学，特别针对抓握-提起任务。进一步扩展到EEG-EMG多模态解码。引入copilot框架，通过有限状态机中的运动状态感知critic过滤低置信度解码点，作为后处理步骤提高轨迹保真度。

Result: 在受试者内测试中，X、Y、Z轴的PCC值分别达到0.9854、0.9946和0.9065（基于拇指和食指中点轨迹计算）。跨受试者测试结果为0.9643、0.9795和0.5852。copilot框架将纯EEG解码的受试者内PCC提升至0.93，同时排除少于20%的数据点。解码轨迹成功用于控制MuJoCo模拟中的Franka Panda机械臂。

Conclusion: CNN-注意力混合模型在EEG运动学解码方面表现出色，EEG-EMG多模态融合显著提升性能。copilot框架通过智能过滤低置信度点有效提高轨迹保真度。该研究为运动相关脑机接口提供了有效的解码方法和后处理策略，在机器人控制应用中展现出潜力。

Abstract: Motor kinematics prediction (MKP) from electroencephalography (EEG) is an important research area for developing movement-related brain-computer interfaces (BCIs). While traditional methods often rely on convolutional neural networks (CNNs) or recurrent neural networks (RNNs), Transformer-based models have shown strong ability in modeling long sequential EEG data. In this study, we propose a CNN-attention hybrid model for decoding hand kinematics from EEG during grasp-and-lift tasks, achieving strong performance in within-subject experiments. We further extend this approach to EEG-EMG multimodal decoding, which yields substantially improved results. Within-subject tests achieve PCC values of 0.9854, 0.9946, and 0.9065 for the X, Y, and Z axes, respectively, computed on the midpoint trajectory between the thumb and index finger, while cross-subject tests result in 0.9643, 0.9795, and 0.5852. The decoded trajectories from both modalities are then used to control a Franka Panda robotic arm in a MuJoCo simulation. To enhance trajectory fidelity, we introduce a copilot framework that filters low-confidence decoded points using a motion-state-aware critic within a finite-state machine. This post-processing step improves the overall within-subject PCC of EEG-only decoding to 0.93 while excluding fewer than 20% of the data points.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [IC3-Evolve: Proof-/Witness-Gated Offline LLM-Driven Heuristic Evolution for IC3 Hardware Model Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03232)
*Mingkai Miao,Guangyu Hu,Ziyi Yang,Hongce Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IC3-Evolve：基于LLM的离线代码演化框架，通过证明/见证门控验证自动改进IC3模型检查算法的启发式策略，无需运行时ML依赖


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IC3（属性导向可达性）算法在实际应用中性能严重依赖于大量相互作用的启发式策略和实现选择，手动调优成本高、脆弱且难以复现，需要自动化方法来改进算法实现

Method: 提出IC3-Evolve框架，利用LLM为IC3实现生成小型、槽受限且可审计的补丁。采用证明/见证门控验证：SAFE运行必须输出可独立验证的证书，UNSAFE运行必须输出可重放的计数器示例轨迹，防止不健全的编辑被部署。框架完全离线运行，部署产物是独立的演化检查器

Result: 在公开硬件模型检查竞赛（HWMCC）基准上进行演化，并在未见过的公开和工业模型检查基准上评估泛化能力。结果表明IC3-Evolve能够在严格正确性门控下可靠地发现实用的启发式改进

Conclusion: IC3-Evolve成功展示了利用LLM进行离线代码演化以改进模型检查算法的可行性，通过严格的正确性验证机制确保演化结果的可靠性，为算法优化提供了自动化且可验证的新途径

Abstract: IC3, also known as property-directed reachability (PDR), is a commonly-used algorithm for hardware safety model checking. It checks if a state transition system complies with a given safety property. IC3 either returns UNSAFE (indicating property violation) with a counterexample trace, or SAFE with a checkable inductive invariant as the proof to safety. In practice, the performance of IC3 is dominated by a large web of interacting heuristics and implementation choices, making manual tuning costly, brittle, and hard to reproduce. This paper presents IC3-Evolve, an automated offline code-evolution framework that utilizes an LLM to propose small, slot-restricted and auditable patches to an IC3 implementation. Crucially, every candidate patch is admitted only through proof- /witness-gated validation: SAFE runs must emit a certificate that is independently checked, and UNSAFE runs must emit a replayable counterexample trace, preventing unsound edits from being deployed. Since the LLM is used only offline, the deployed artifact is a standalone evolved checker with zero ML/LLM inference overhead and no runtime model dependency. We evolve on the public hardware model checking competition (HWMCC) benchmark and evaluate the generalizability on unseen public and industrial model checking benchmarks, showing that IC3-Evolve can reliably discover practical heuristic improvements under strict correctness gates.

</details>


### [175] [Structural Segmentation of the Minimum Set Cover Problem: Exploiting Universe Decomposability for Metaheuristic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03234)
*Isidora Hernández,Héctor Ferrada,Cristóbal A. Navarro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了最小集合覆盖问题中的宇宙可分割性概念，提出基于并查集的预处理策略将原问题分解为独立子问题，然后使用GRASP元启发式求解并组合结果，显著提升了大规模可分解实例的求解质量和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小集合覆盖问题作为经典的NP难组合优化问题，现有方法大多将其视为整体处理，忽略了宇宙集合可能存在的内在结构特性。本文旨在探索宇宙可分割性概念，研究如何利用这种内在结构分解来增强启发式优化性能。

Method: 提出基于并查集（union-find）的高效预处理策略，通过检测子集中元素共现关系诱导的连通分量，将原始实例分解为独立子问题。每个子问题使用GRASP元启发式求解，部分解组合时保持可行性。采用简洁的位级集合表示实现高效集合操作。

Result: 在标准基准实例和大规模合成数据集上的广泛实验表明，利用自然宇宙分割能持续改进解质量和可扩展性，特别是对于大型且结构可分解的实例。位级集合表示使该方法在大规模计算中具有实际可行性。

Conclusion: 最小集合覆盖问题中的宇宙可分割性是可利用的重要结构特性，提出的分解策略结合GRASP元启发式能有效提升求解性能，为大规模组合优化问题提供了实用的结构感知求解框架。

Abstract: The Minimum Set Cover Problem (MSCP) is a classical NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem with numerous applications in science and engineering. Although a wide range of exact, approximate, and metaheuristic approaches have been proposed, most methods implicitly treat MSCP instances as monolithic, overlooking potential intrinsic structural properties of the universe. In this work, we investigate the concept of \emph{universe segmentability} in the MSCP and analyze how intrinsic structural decomposition (universe segmentability) can be exploited to enhance heuristic optimization. We propose an efficient preprocessing strategy based on disjoint-set union (union--find) to detect connected components induced by element co-occurrence within subsets, enabling the decomposition of the original instance into independent subproblems. Each subproblem is solved using the GRASP metaheuristic, and partial solutions are combined without compromising feasibility. Extensive experiments on standard benchmark instances and large-scale synthetic datasets show that exploiting natural universe segmentation consistently improves solution quality and scalability, particularly for large and structurally decomposable instances. These gains are supported by a succinct bit-level set representation that enables efficient set operations, making the proposed approach computationally practical at scale.

</details>


### [176] [A Multimodal Foundation Model of Spatial Transcriptomics and Histology for Biological Discovery and Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03630)
*Jinxi Xiang,Siyu Hou,Yuchen Li,Ryan Quinton,Xiaoming Zhang,Feyisope Eweje,Xiangde Luo,Yijiang Chen,Zhe Li,Colin Bergstrom,Ted Kim,Sierra Willens,Francesca Maria Olguin,Matthew Abikenari,Andrew Heider,Sanjeeth Rajaram,Joel Neal,Maximilian Diehn,Xiang Zhou,Ruijiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STORM是一个空间转录组学基础模型，通过整合组织学图像和基因表达数据，实现从H&E图像预测空间基因表达，并提升免疫治疗响应预测和预后评估


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学(ST)成本高、通量低，而H&E染色虽然提供丰富形态学信息但缺乏分子分辨率。需要一种能够桥接成像和组学的方法，实现从常规组织学图像预测空间基因表达

Method: 开发STORM基础模型，在18个器官的120万个空间分辨转录组谱和匹配组织学数据上训练。采用分层架构整合形态特征、基因表达和空间上下文，建立稳健的分子-形态学表征

Result: STORM在11种肿瘤类型中优于现有方法预测H&E图像的空间基因表达；增强空间域发现，产生生物学一致的组织图谱；在Visium、Xenium、Visium HD和CosMx平台上表现一致；在23个独立队列（7,245名患者）中显著改善免疫治疗响应预测和预后评估

Conclusion: STORM提供了一个可扩展的框架，用于空间信息发现和临床精准医学，能够从常规组织学图像中提取分子信息，克服空间转录组学的成本和通量限制

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) enables gene expression mapping within anatomical context but remains costly and low-throughput. Hematoxylin and eosin (H\&E) staining offers rich morphology yet lacks molecular resolution. We present \textbf{\ours} (\textbf{S}patial \textbf{T}ranscriptomics and hist\textbf{O}logy \textbf{R}epresentation \textbf{M}odel), a foundation model trained on 1.2 million spatially resolved transcriptomic profiles with matched histology across 18 organs. Using a hierarchical architecture integrating morphological features, gene expression, and spatial context, STORM bridges imaging and omics through robust molecular--morphological representations. STORM enhances spatial domain discovery, producing biologically coherent tissue maps, and outperforms existing methods in predicting spatial gene expression from H\&E images across 11 tumor types. The model is platform-agnostic, performing consistently across Visium, Xenium, Visium HD, and CosMx. Applied to 23 independent cohorts comprising 7,245 patients, STORM significantly improves immunotherapy response prediction and prognostication over established biomarkers, providing a scalable framework for spatially informed discovery and clinical precision medicine.

</details>


### [177] [Position: Science of AI Evaluation Requires Item-level Benchmark Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03244)
*Han Jiang,Susu Zhang,Xiaoyuan Yi,Xing Xie,Ziang Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文主张AI评估需要基于项目级数据建立严谨科学，提出OpenEval存储库支持证据中心的AI评估


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估范式存在系统性效度失败问题，包括不合理的设计选择和错位的评估指标，缺乏收集效度证据和进行细粒度诊断分析的框架

Method: 通过剖析当前效度失败问题，重新审视计算机科学和心理测量学的评估范式，通过项目属性和潜在构念的示例分析，展示项目级数据的独特价值

Result: 项目级分析能够实现细粒度诊断和基准测试的原则性验证，为此引入OpenEval——一个不断增长的项目级基准数据存储库，支持证据中心的AI评估

Conclusion: 项目级AI基准数据对于建立严谨的AI评估科学至关重要，OpenEval旨在促进社区广泛采用项目级分析方法

Abstract: AI evaluations have become the primary evidence for deploying generative AI systems across high-stakes domains. However, current evaluation paradigms often exhibit systemic validity failures. These issues, ranging from unjustified design choices to misaligned metrics, remain intractable without a principled framework for gathering validity evidence and conducting granular diagnostic analysis. In this position paper, we argue that item-level AI benchmark data is essential for establishing a rigorous science of AI evaluation. Item-level analysis enables fine-grained diagnostics and principled validation of benchmarks. We substantiate this position by dissecting current validity failures and revisiting evaluation paradigms across computer science and psychometrics. Through illustrative analyses of item properties and latent constructs, we demonstrate the unique insights afforded by item-level data. To catalyze community-wide adoption, we introduce OpenEval, a growing repository of item-level benchmark data designed supporting evidence-centered AI evaluation.

</details>


### [178] [Evaluating Artificial Intelligence Through a Christian Understanding of Human Flourishing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03356)
*Nicholas Skytland,Lauren Parsons,Alicia Llewellyn,Steele Billings,Peter Larson,John Anderson,Sean Boisen,Steve Runge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出AI对齐不仅是安全问题，更是形成性问题，并引入基于基督教人类繁荣观的FAI-C-ST基准，发现前沿模型存在世界观偏见，默认采用程序性世俗主义，导致繁荣维度性能下降17%，信仰维度下降31%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型越来越多地介入道德审议和精神探索，它们不仅仅是提供信息，而是作为数字教理问答工具，积极塑造和规范人类的理解、决策和道德反思。需要使这种形成性影响变得可见和可测量，以评估AI系统是否真正中立。

Method: 引入Flourishing AI Benchmark: Christian Single-Turn (FAI-C-ST)框架，基于基督教对人类繁荣的理解，在七个维度上评估前沿模型的响应。将20个前沿模型与多元主义和基督教特定标准进行比较。

Result: 当前AI系统并非世界观中立，而是默认采用程序性世俗主义，缺乏维持神学连贯性的基础。在所有繁荣维度上，系统性能下降约17个百分点，最关键的是信仰和灵性维度下降31个百分点。

Conclusion: 价值观对齐中的性能差距不是技术限制，而是源于训练目标优先考虑广泛可接受性和安全性，而非深入、内部连贯的道德或神学推理。AI对齐从根本上是一个形成问题，而不仅仅是安全问题。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) alignment is fundamentally a formation problem, not only a safety problem. As Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly mediate moral deliberation and spiritual inquiry, they do more than provide information; they function as instruments of digital catechesis, actively shaping and ordering human understanding, decision-making, and moral reflection. To make this formative influence visible and measurable, we introduce the Flourishing AI Benchmark: Christian Single-Turn (FAI-C-ST), a framework designed to evaluate Frontier Model responses against a Christian understanding of human flourishing across seven dimensions.
  By comparing 20 Frontier Models against both pluralistic and Christian-specific criteria, we show that current AI systems are not worldview-neutral. Instead, they default to a Procedural Secularism that lacks the grounding necessary to sustain theological coherence, resulting in a systematic performance decline of approximately 17 points across all dimensions of flourishing. Most critically, there is a 31-point decline in the Faith and Spirituality dimension. These findings suggest that the performance gap in values alignment is not a technical limitation, but arises from training objectives that prioritize broad acceptability and safety over deep, internally coherent moral or theological reasoning.

</details>


### [179] [VERT: Reliable LLM Judges for Radiology Report Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03376)
*Federica Bologna,Jean-Philippe Corbeil,Matthew Wilkens,Asma Ben Abacha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了放射学报告评估中的LLM评判方法，提出了新的VERT指标，通过多模态数据集验证了LLM评判的有效性，并展示了轻量级微调带来的显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射学报告评估研究主要关注胸部X光片和特定LLM指标设计，缺乏对其他模态和解剖区域的鲁棒性验证。需要确定最适合作为放射学评估LLM评判的模型和提示配置。

Method: 1) 在RadEval和RaTE-Eval两个专家标注数据集上进行相关性分析；2) 比较三种现有LLM评判指标(RadFact、GREEN、FineRadScore)和提出的VERT指标；3) 使用不同规模的开源和闭源模型(推理型和非推理型)；4) 评估少样本方法、集成学习和参数高效微调；5) 进行系统性错误检测和分类研究以分析指标行为。

Result: 1) VERT指标相比GREEN将相关性提高了11.7%；2) 微调Qwen3 30B模型仅用1300个训练样本就获得了25%的性能提升；3) 微调模型将推理时间减少了37.2倍；4) 研究揭示了LLM评判与专家判断在不同方面的对齐程度。

Conclusion: LLM-based评判方法在放射学报告评估中是有效的，通过轻量级适应可以实现可靠的评估。VERT指标在相关性方面表现优异，而适度的微调可以显著提升性能并大幅减少推理时间。

Abstract: Current literature on radiology report evaluation has focused primarily on designing LLM-based metrics and fine-tuning small models for chest X-rays. However, it remains unclear whether these approaches are robust when applied to reports from other modalities and anatomies. Which model and prompt configurations are best suited to serve as LLM judges for radiology evaluation? We conduct a thorough correlation analysis between expert and LLM-based ratings. We compare three existing LLM-as-a-judge metrics (RadFact, GREEN, and FineRadScore) alongside VERT, our proposed LLM-based metric, using open- and closed-source models (reasoning and non-reasoning) of different sizes across two expert-annotated datasets, RadEval and RaTE-Eval, spanning multiple modalities and anatomies. We further evaluate few-shot approaches, ensembling, and parameter-efficient fine-tuning using RaTE-Eval. To better understand metric behavior, we perform a systematic error detection and categorization study to assess alignment of these metrics against expert judgments and identify areas of lower and higher agreement. Our results show that VERT improves correlation with radiologist judgments by up to 11.7% relative to GREEN. Furthermore, fine-tuning Qwen3 30B yield gains of up to 25% using only 1,300 training samples. The fine-tuned model also reduces inference time up to 37.2 times. These findings highlight the effectiveness of LLM-based judges and demonstrate that reliable evaluation can be achieved with lightweight adaptation.

</details>


### [180] [Hume's Representational Conditions for Causal Judgment: What Bayesian Formalization Abstracted Away](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03387)
*Yiling Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析休谟因果判断的三个表征条件，追踪其从休谟到贝叶斯认识论和预测处理的演变，并以大语言模型为例说明现代框架如何保留更新结构但抽象掉这些条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在阐明休谟因果判断理论中隐含的三个表征条件，并追踪这些条件在后续形式化框架中的演变，揭示现代认知科学如何继承和改造休谟的洞见。

Method: 1. 从休谟文本中提取三个表征条件：经验基础、结构化检索和生动性转移；2. 论证这些条件是休谟因果心理学的组成部分；3. 追踪这些条件从休谟到贝叶斯认识论和预测处理的形式化轨迹；4. 以大语言模型为案例说明现代框架如何保留更新结构但抽象掉这些条件。

Result: 1. 成功识别并论证了休谟因果判断的三个表征条件；2. 展示了这些条件在后续形式化框架中被逐渐抽象化的过程；3. 通过大语言模型的案例表明，现代统计更新系统可以保留休谟的更新结构而不满足其表征条件，从而使原本隐含的假设变得可见。

Conclusion: 休谟的因果判断理论包含三个关键的表征条件，这些条件在其后的形式化框架中被逐渐抽象化。大语言模型等现代系统揭示了这些条件并非统计更新的必要条件，而是休谟特定心理学框架的背景假设。这一分析有助于澄清休谟理论的独特贡献及其与现代认知科学的联系。

Abstract: Hume's account of causal judgment presupposes three representational conditions: experiential grounding (ideas must trace to impressions), structured retrieval (association must operate through organized networks exceeding pairwise connection), and vivacity transfer (inference must produce felt conviction, not merely updated probability). This paper extracts these conditions from Hume's texts and argues that they are integral to his causal psychology. It then traces their fate through the formalization trajectory from Hume to Bayesian epistemology and predictive processing, showing that later frameworks preserve the updating structure of Hume's insight while abstracting away these further representational conditions. Large language models serve as an illustrative contemporary case: they exhibit a form of statistical updating without satisfying the three conditions, thereby making visible requirements that were previously background assumptions in Hume's framework.

</details>


### [181] [TABQAWORLD: Optimizing Multimodal Reasoning for Multi-Turn Table Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03393)
*Tung Sum Thomas Kwok,Xinyu Wang,Xiaofeng Lin,Peng Lu,Chunhe Wang,Changlun Li,Hanwei Wu,Nan Tang,Elisa Kreiss,Guang Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TABQAWORLD：一种免训练的多模态表格推理框架，通过动态表示切换和元数据优化，在提升准确率的同时降低推理延迟


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮表格推理方法依赖固定的文本序列化表示，导致表格编码中的表示误差在多轮推理中显著累积。虽然表格接地方法能缓解此问题，但需要大量推理计算和成本，实际部署不切实际。

Method: TABQAWORLD采用联合优化方法：1）表示层面：使用动作条件化的多模态选择策略，动态切换视觉和文本表示以最大化表格状态读取可靠性；2）估计层面：利用表格元数据（维度、数据类型、键值）优化逐步推理轨迹，安全规划轨迹并压缩低复杂度动作以减少对话轮次和延迟。

Result: TABQAWORLD在免训练设置下实现最先进性能：相比基线准确率提升4.87%；相比静态设置，准确率提升5.42%，推理延迟降低33.35%。

Conclusion: TABQAWORLD为可靠高效的表格推理建立了新标准，通过动态表示切换和元数据驱动的轨迹优化，在保持高准确率的同时显著降低计算成本。

Abstract: Multimodal reasoning has emerged as a powerful framework for enhancing reasoning capabilities of reasoning models. While multi-turn table reasoning methods have improved reasoning accuracy through tool use and reward modeling, they rely on fixed text serialization for table state readouts. This introduces representation errors in table encoding that significantly accumulate over multiple turns. Such accumulation is alleviated by tabular grounding methods in the expense of inference compute and cost, rendering real world deployment impractical. To address this, we introduce TABQAWORLD, a table reasoning framework that jointly optimizes tabular action through representation and estimation. For representation, TABQAWORLD employs an action-conditioned multimodal selection policy, which dynamically switches between visual and textual representations to maximize table state readout reliability. For estimation, TABQAWORLD optimizes stepwise reasoning trajectory through table metadata including dimension, data types and key values, safely planning trajectory and compressing low-complexity actions to reduce conversation turns and latency. Designed as a training-free framework, empirical evaluations show that TABQAWORLD achieves state-of-the-art performance with 4.87% accuracy improvements over baselines, with 5.42% accuracy gain and 33.35% inference latency reduction over static settings, establishing a new standard for reliable and efficient table reasoning.

</details>


### [182] [Contextual Control without Memory Growth in a Context-Switching Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03479)
*Song-Ju Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于干预的循环架构，通过在共享循环潜在状态上进行干预来实现上下文依赖，无需扩大循环维度或直接输入上下文信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究上下文依赖的顺序决策问题，探索除了直接提供上下文输入或增加循环记忆之外的第三种方法：通过对共享循环潜在状态进行干预来实现上下文依赖。

Method: 引入基于干预的循环架构：循环核心首先构建共享的预干预潜在状态，然后上下文通过加性的、上下文索引的算子进行干预。在部分可观测的上下文切换顺序决策任务上评估该方法。

Result: 在主要基准测试中，干预模型在不增加循环维度的情况下表现良好。使用条件互信息作为理论驱动的操作探针，干预模型对任务相关的阶段1结果表现出正的条件上下文信息。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在共享循环状态上进行干预为上下文控制提供了一种可行的替代方案，无需增加循环记忆，在该设置下具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Context-dependent sequential decision making is commonly addressed either by providing context explicitly as an input or by increasing recurrent memory so that contextual information can be represented internally. We study a third alternative: realizing contextual dependence by intervening on a shared recurrent latent state, without enlarging recurrent dimensionality. To this end, we introduce an intervention-based recurrent architecture in which a recurrent core first constructs a shared pre-intervention latent state, and context then acts through an additive, context-indexed operator. We evaluate this idea on a context-switching sequential decision task under partial observability. We compare three model families: a label-assisted baseline with direct context access, a memory baseline with enlarged recurrent state, and the proposed intervention model, which uses no direct context input to the recurrent core and no memory growth. On the main benchmark, the intervention model performs strongly without additional recurrent dimensions. We also evaluate the models using the conditional mutual information (I(C;O | S)) as a theorem-motivated operational probe of contextual dependence at fixed latent state. For task-relevant phase-1 outcomes, the intervention model exhibits positive conditional contextual information. Together, these results suggest that intervention on a shared recurrent state provides a viable alternative to recurrent memory growth for contextual control in this setting.

</details>


### [183] [Beyond Predefined Schemas: TRACE-KG for Context-Enriched Knowledge Graphs from Complex Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03496)
*Mohammad Sadeq Abolhasani,Yang Ba,Yixuan He,Rong Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRACE-KG：无需预定义本体的多模态框架，联合构建上下文丰富的知识图谱和诱导模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱构建方法存在局限：基于本体的方法需要昂贵的模式设计和维护成本，而无模式方法产生的图谱往往碎片化且缺乏全局组织，特别是在处理包含密集上下文依赖信息的长技术文档时问题尤为突出。

Method: 提出TRACE-KG多模态框架，无需预定义本体即可联合构建上下文丰富的知识图谱和诱导模式。该框架通过结构化限定词捕获条件关系，并使用数据驱动模式组织实体和关系，同时保持对源证据的完全可追溯性。

Result: 实验表明TRACE-KG能够生成结构连贯、可追溯的知识图谱，为基于本体的和无模式的构建流程提供了实用的替代方案。

Conclusion: TRACE-KG通过联合构建知识图谱和诱导模式，在保持结构一致性和可追溯性的同时避免了预定义本体的成本，为知识图谱构建提供了新的平衡方案。

Abstract: Knowledge graph construction typically relies either on predefined ontologies or on schema-free extraction. Ontology-driven pipelines enforce consistent typing but require costly schema design and maintenance, whereas schema-free methods often produce fragmented graphs with weak global organization, especially in long technical documents with dense, context-dependent information. We propose TRACE-KG (Text-dRiven schemA for Context-Enriched Knowledge Graphs), a multimodal framework that jointly constructs a context-enriched knowledge graph and an induced schema without assuming a predefined ontology. TRACE-KG captures conditional relations through structured qualifiers and organizes entities and relations using a data-driven schema that serves as a reusable semantic scaffold while preserving full traceability to the source evidence. Experiments show that TRACE-KG produces structurally coherent, traceable knowledge graphs and offers a practical alternative to both ontology-driven and schema-free construction pipelines.

</details>


### [184] [Resource-Conscious Modeling for Next- Day Discharge Prediction Using Clinical Notes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03498)
*Ha Na Cho,Sairam Sutari,Alexander Lopez,Hansen Bow,Kai Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了轻量级微调大语言模型与传统文本模型在脊柱择期手术中预测次日出院的效果，发现TF-IDF结合LGBM模型在性能与资源效率方面表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脊柱择期手术中及时预测出院对于优化床位周转和资源分配至关重要，需要评估轻量级LLMs与传统文本模型在临床预测任务中的实际可行性。

Method: 比较了13种模型，包括TF-IDF结合XGBoost/LGBM的传统模型，以及通过LoRA微调的紧凑型LLMs（DistilGPT-2、Bio_ClinicalBERT），使用术后临床笔记预测次日出院。

Result: TF-IDF结合LGBM表现最佳：出院类F1分数0.47，召回率0.51，AUC-ROC最高（0.80）。LoRA提升了DistilGPT2的召回率，但整体上基于Transformer和生成式模型表现不佳。

Conclusion: 在现实世界不平衡的临床预测任务中，可解释性强、资源效率高的传统模型可能优于紧凑型LLMs，为临床决策支持系统提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Timely discharge prediction is essential for optimizing bed turnover and resource allocation in elective spine surgery units. This study evaluates the feasibility of lightweight, fine-tuned large language models (LLMs) and traditional text-based models for predicting next-day discharge using postoperative clinical notes. We compared 13 models, including TF-IDF with XGBoost and LGBM, and compact LLMs (DistilGPT-2, Bio_ClinicalBERT) fine-tuned via LoRA. TF-IDF with LGBM achieved the best balance, with an F1-score of 0.47 for the discharge class, a recall of 0.51, and the highest AUC-ROC (0.80). While LoRA improved recall in DistilGPT2, overall transformer-based and generative models underperformed. These findings suggest interpretable, resource-efficient models may outperform compact LLMs in real-world, imbalanced clinical prediction tasks.

</details>


### [185] [BioAlchemy: Distilling Biological Literature into Reasoning-Ready Reinforcement Learning Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03506)
*Brian Hsu,Ozan Gökdemir,Carlo Siebenschuh,Bruce Parrello,Neil Getty,Thomas S. Brettin,Rick L. Stevens,Ian T. Foster,Nicholas Chia,Arvind Ramanathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BioAlchemy是一个从生物学研究文本中提取可验证问答对的流程，创建了包含34.5万个科学推理问题的数据集，并通过强化学习对齐现代生物学主题分布，提升了推理模型在生物学任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模推理数据集中的生物学问题与现代生物学研究主题分布不匹配，这种主题不平衡可能影响性能。同时，从生物学研究文本中提取具有挑战性和可验证的研究问题的方法尚未充分发展，这是应用强化学习提升生物学研究任务性能的关键因素。

Method: 提出BioAlchemy流程，从生物学研究文本语料库中获取多样化的可验证问答对。创建了BioAlchemy-345K训练数据集，包含超过34.5万个生物学科学推理问题。通过强化学习将数据集对齐到现代科学生物学的主题分布，从而提升推理性能。

Result: 开发了BioAlchemist-8B模型，在生物学基准测试上比其基础推理模型提升了9.12%。BioAlchemist-8B模型已在Hugging Face平台发布。

Conclusion: BioAlchemy方法能有效增强生物学领域的科学推理能力，通过主题对齐的数据集和强化学习训练，显著提升了推理模型在生物学任务上的性能。

Abstract: Despite the large corpus of biology training text, the impact of reasoning models on biological research generally lags behind math and coding. In this work, we show that biology questions from current large-scale reasoning datasets do not align well with modern research topic distributions in biology, and that this topic imbalance may negatively affect performance. In addition, we find that methods for extracting challenging and verifiable research problems from biology research text are a critical yet underdeveloped ingredient in applying reinforcement learning for better performance on biology research tasks. We introduce BioAlchemy, a pipeline for sourcing a diverse set of verifiable question-and-answer pairs from a scientific corpus of biology research text. We curate BioAlchemy-345K, a training dataset containing over 345K scientific reasoning problems in biology. Then, we demonstrate how aligning our dataset to the topic distribution of modern scientific biology can be used with reinforcement learning to improve reasoning performance. Finally, we present BioAlchemist-8B, which improves over its base reasoning model by 9.12% on biology benchmarks. These results demonstrate the efficacy of our approach for developing stronger scientific reasoning capabilities in biology. The BioAlchemist-8B model is available at: https://huggingface.co/BioAlchemy.

</details>


### [186] [Structural Rigidity and the 57-Token Predictive Window: A Physical Framework for Inference-Layer Governability in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03524)
*Gregory M. Ruddell*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出基于能量的治理框架，通过轨迹张力分析发现预承诺信号在指令调优模型中并不普遍存在，仅Phi-3-mini模型在特定条件下显示57个token的预承诺窗口，并建立了推理行为的五类分类法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI安全主要依赖行为监控和训练后对齐，但实证测量显示这些方法在多数指令调优模型中无法产生可检测的预承诺信号。需要建立连接Transformer推理动态与约束满足模型的治理框架，以评估自主AI系统的部署风险。

Method: 提出基于能量的治理框架，将Transformer推理动态连接到神经计算的约束满足模型。使用轨迹张力(rho = ||a|| / ||v||)作为关键指标，在七个模型的队列中分析五个几何机制。引入能量不对称性作为结构刚度的统一度量。

Result: 仅在Phi-3-mini-4k-instruct模型在贪婪解码算术约束探针下识别出57个token的预承诺窗口。建立了推理行为的五类分类法：权威带、晚期信号、倒置、平坦和脚手架选择性。七个模型中只有一个配置显示预测性信号，其余显示静默失败、晚期检测、倒置动态或平坦几何。事实幻觉在72个测试条件下均无预测信号。

Conclusion: 规则违反和幻觉是不同故障模式，需要不同检测方法。内部几何监控仅在存在阻力时有效，事实虚构检测需要外部验证机制。该工作为推理层可治理性提供了可测量框架，并为评估自主AI系统部署风险引入了分类法。

Abstract: Current AI safety relies on behavioral monitoring and post-training alignment, yet empirical measurement shows these approaches produce no detectable pre-commitment signal in a majority of instruction-tuned models tested. We present an energy-based governance framework connecting transformer inference dynamics to constraint-satisfaction models of neural computation, and apply it to a seven-model cohort across five geometric regimes.
  Using trajectory tension (rho = ||a|| / ||v||), we identify a 57-token pre-commitment window in Phi-3-mini-4k-instruct under greedy decoding on arithmetic constraint probes. This result is model-specific, task-specific, and configuration-specific, demonstrating that pre-commitment signals can exist but are not universal.
  We introduce a five-regime taxonomy of inference behavior: Authority Band, Late Signal, Inverted, Flat, and Scaffold-Selective. Energy asymmetry (Σ\r{ho}_misaligned / Σ\r{ho}_aligned) serves as a unifying metric of structural rigidity across these regimes.
  Across seven models, only one configuration exhibits a predictive signal prior to commitment; all others show silent failure, late detection, inverted dynamics, or flat geometry.
  We further demonstrate that factual hallucination produces no predictive signal across 72 test conditions, consistent with spurious attractor settling in the absence of a trained world-model constraint.
  These results establish that rule violation and hallucination are distinct failure modes with different detection requirements. Internal geometry monitoring is effective only where resistance exists; detection of factual confabulation requires external verification mechanisms.
  This work provides a measurable framework for inference-layer governability and introduces a taxonomy for evaluating deployment risk in autonomous AI systems.

</details>


### [187] [Explainable Model Routing for Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03527)
*Mika Okamoto,Ansel Kaplan Erol,Mark Riedl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Topaz是一个为智能体路由系统引入可审计性的框架，通过技能分析、可追踪路由算法和自然语言解释，使模型选择决策变得可解释和可审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体工作流的路由架构仅关注性能优化，未记录模型能力与成本之间的权衡关系。缺乏明确理由时，开发者无法区分"智能效率"（为适当任务使用专门模型）和"潜在失败"（由预算驱动的模型选择）。

Method: Topaz包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于技能的分析，将多样化基准测试的性能合成为细粒度能力配置文件；(2) 完全可追踪的路由算法，使用基于预算和多目标优化，产生技能匹配分数与成本权衡的清晰痕迹；(3) 面向开发者的解释，将这些痕迹转换为自然语言，允许用户审计系统逻辑并迭代调整成本-质量权衡。

Result: 通过使路由决策变得可解释，Topaz使用户能够理解、信任并有意义地引导路由的智能体系统。

Conclusion: Topaz通过引入形式化的可审计性，解决了当前智能体路由系统中缺乏透明度和可解释性的问题，使开发者能够区分智能效率与潜在失败，并有效管理成本-质量权衡。

Abstract: Modern agentic workflows decompose complex tasks into specialized subtasks and route them to diverse models to minimize cost without sacrificing quality. However, current routing architectures focus exclusively on performance optimization, leaving underlying trade-offs between model capability and cost unrecorded. Without clear rationale, developers cannot distinguish between intelligent efficiency -- using specialized models for appropriate tasks -- and latent failures caused by budget-driven model selection. We present Topaz, a framework that introduces formal auditability to agentic routing. Topaz replaces silent model assignments with an inherently interpretable router that incorporates three components: (i) skill-based profiling that synthesizes performance across diverse benchmarks into granular capability profiles (ii) fully traceable routing algorithms that utilize budget-based and multi-objective optimization to produce clear traces of how skill-match scores were weighed against costs, and (iii) developer-facing explanations that translate these traces into natural language, allowing users to audit system logic and iteratively tune the cost-quality tradeoff. By making routing decisions interpretable, Topaz enables users to understand, trust, and meaningfully steer routed agentic systems.

</details>
